# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title joseph Model

class DirectoryFilter {
    ignoreDirs : List<String>
    isIgnored(path : String) : boolean
    isIgnoredPath(path : Path) : boolean
}
class DirectoryToMarkdown {
    directoryFilter : DirectoryFilter
    fileContentReader : FileContentReader
    markdownWriter : MarkdownWriter
    convert(options : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions) : MarkdownConversionResult
    createDefaultIgnoreDirs() : List<String>
    createDefaultOptions() : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
    createMarkdownFileEntry(relativePath : Path, extension : String, content : String) : MarkdownFileEntry
    createOptions(directoryPath : Path, outputFile : Path, fileTypes : List<String>, ignoreDirs : List<String>, recursive : boolean, maxFileSizeMb : int) : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
    directoryToMarkdown(directoryPath : Path, outputFile : Path, fileTypes : List<String>, ignoreDirs : List<String>, recursive : boolean, maxFileSizeMb : int) : int
    getExtension(path : Path) : String
    isIgnored(path : String, ignoreDirs : List<String>) : boolean
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String) : String
    readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : String
    readFileSafelyfromPath(filePath : Path) : String
    readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : String
    shouldIncludeExtension(extension : String, fileTypes : List<String>) : boolean
    walkDirectory(currentDir : Path, md : BufferedWriter, optons : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, baseDir : Path, result : MarkdownConversionResult) : void
}
class DirectoryToMarkdownOptions {
    directoryPath : String
    fileTypes : List<String>
    ignoreDirs : List<String>
    maxFileSizeMb : int
    outputFile : String
    recursive : boolean
}
class FileContentReader {
    readFileSafely(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
}
class FileReadResult {
    content : String
    readable : boolean
    skippedReason : SkipReason
}
class MarkdownConversionResult {
    processedFileCount : int
    processedFiles : List<MarkdownFileEntry>
    addProcessedFile(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : void
}
class MarkdownFileEntry {
    content : String
    extension : String
    language : String
    relativePath : String
}
class MarkdownWriter {
    formatEntry(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : String
}
enum SkipReason {
    NONE
    TOO_LARGE
    UNREADABLE
}

DirectoryToMarkdown --> DirectoryFilter : directoryFilter
DirectoryToMarkdown --> FileContentReader : fileContentReader
DirectoryToMarkdown --> MarkdownWriter : markdownWriter
MarkdownConversionResult *-- "*" MarkdownFileEntry : processedFiles
@enduml
```

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

```plantuml
    directoryPath : String

    ignoreDirs : List<String>
    maxFileSizeMb : int
    outputFile : String
    recursive : boolean
}
class FileContentReader {
    readFileSafely(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
}

    content : String
    readable : boolean
    skippedReason : SkipReason
}

    processedFileCount : int

}

    content : String
    extension : String
    language : String
    relativePath : String
}

    formatEntry(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : String

    NONE
    TOO_LARGE
    UNREADABLE
}
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryFilter::isIgnored
Summary:
Determines whether a directory path should be ignored by checking whether any
path component matches one of the configured ignored directory names.
Algorithm:
1. Split the input path into components using the platform path separator or
   normalized "/" semantics.
2. For each component, compare it against each string in ignoreDirs.
3. Return true if any component equals an ignored directory name exactly.
4. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- ignoreDirs
- isIgnoredPath(path : Path) : boolean
Related Classes: None
Input:
- path: A path string representing a directory or file path. It may be absolute
  or relative.
- The comparison is exact on path components, not substring-based.
Output:
- boolean: true if any path component is listed in ignoreDirs; otherwise false.
Pre-condition:
- ignoreDirs is initialized.
- path is not null and represents a valid path string.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- The return value reflects whether the path contains an ignored directory name
  as a path component.

```plantuml
    directoryToMarkdown(directoryPath : Path, outputFile : Path, fileTypes : List<String>, ignoreDirs : List<String>, recursive : boolean, maxFileSizeMb : int) : int
    getExtension(path : Path) : String
    isIgnored(path : String, ignoreDirs : List<String>) : boolean
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String) : String
    readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : String
    readFileSafelyfromPath(filePath : Path) : String
    readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : String
    shouldIncludeExtension(extension : String, fileTypes : List<String>) : boolean
    walkDirectory(currentDir : Path, md : BufferedWriter, optons : DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, baseDir : Path, result : MarkdownConversionResult) : void
}
class DirectoryToMarkdownOptions {
    directoryPath : String
    fileTypes : List<String>
    ignoreDirs : List<String>
    maxFileSizeMb : int
    outputFile : String
    recursive : boolean
}
class FileContentReader {
    readFileSafely(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
}
class FileReadResult {
    content : String
    readable : boolean
    skippedReason : SkipReason
}
class MarkdownConversionResult {
    processedFileCount : int
    processedFiles : List<MarkdownFileEntry>
    addProcessedFile(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : void
}
class MarkdownFileEntry {
    content : String
    extension : String
    language : String
    relativePath : String
}
class MarkdownWriter {
    formatEntry(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : String
}
enum SkipReason {
    NONE
    TOO_LARGE
    UNREADABLE
}
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryFilter::isIgnored
Summary:
Determines whether a directory path should be ignored by checking whether any
path component matches one of the configured ignored directory names.
Algorithm:
1. Split the input path into components using the platform path separator or
   normalized "/" semantics.
2. For each component, compare it against each string in ignoreDirs.
3. Return true if any component equals an ignored directory name exactly.
4. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- ignoreDirs
- isIgnoredPath(path : Path) : boolean
Related Classes: None
Input:
- path: A path string representing a directory or file path. It may be absolute
  or relative.
- The comparison is exact on path components, not substring-based.
Output:
- boolean: true if any path component is listed in ignoreDirs; otherwise false.
Pre-condition:
- ignoreDirs is initialized.
- path is not null and represents a valid path string.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- The return value reflects whether the path contains an ignored directory name
  as a path component.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Operation Specifications
MarkdownConversionResult::addProcessedFile
Summary:
Adds a processed Markdown file entry to the result and keeps the processed file
count consistent with the stored entry list.
Algorithm:
1. Append the entry to processedFiles.
2. Update processedFileCount to equal the new size of processedFiles.
3. Return.
Related Features:
- processedFileCount
- processedFiles
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input:
- entry: A MarkdownFileEntry representing one successfully processed file.
Output:
- void
Pre-condition:
- entry is not null.
- processedFiles is initialized.
Post-condition:
- entry is present in processedFiles.
- processedFileCount equals processedFiles.size().

# Operation Specifications
FileContentReader::readFileSafelyFromString
Summary:
Reads the contents of a file identified by a string path as UTF-8 text if the
file is within the specified size limit and readable.
Algorithm:
1. Convert the string path to a filesystem Path.
2. Delegate to readFileSafely(Path, maxSizeMb).
3. Return the delegated FileReadResult.
Related Features:
- readFileSafely(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
Related Classes: FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- filePath: String representation of a file path.
- maxSizeMb: Maximum allowed file size in megabytes.
Output:
- FileReadResult: Structured read outcome with content, readable flag, and
  skip reason.
Pre-condition:
- filePath is not null.
- maxSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- The returned result is equivalent to calling the Path-based reader on the
  converted path.

FileContentReader::readFileSafely
Summary:
Reads the contents of a file as UTF-8 text if the file is within the specified
size limit and readable; otherwise returns a structured skip result.
Algorithm:
1. Verify that the file exists and can be accessed.
2. Compare its size against maxSizeMb converted to bytes.
3. If size exceeds the limit, return a FileReadResult marked unreadable = false
   and skippedReason = TOO_LARGE.
4. Attempt to read the file as UTF-8 text.
5. If read succeeds, return readable = true, skippedReason = NONE, and content.
6. If read fails, return readable = false, skippedReason = UNREADABLE.
Related Features:
- readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
Related Classes: FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- filePath: A Path to a file.
- maxSizeMb: Maximum allowed file size in megabytes.
Output:
- FileReadResult: Structured result containing content, readability flag, and
  skip reason.
Pre-condition:
- filePath is not null.
- maxSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- The returned result always describes one of: successful read, too large, or
  unreadable.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::isIgnored
Summary:
Checks whether a relative path should be ignored because any of its components
matches one of the configured ignored directory names.
Algorithm:
1. Split the path into components.
2. Compare each component for exact equality with each entry in ignoreDirs.
3. Return true on the first match.
4. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- directoryFilter
- createOptions(...)
Related Classes: None
Input:
- path: Relative or absolute path string.
- ignoreDirs: List of directory names to ignore.
Output:
- boolean: true if the path contains an ignored component; otherwise false.
Pre-condition:
- path is not null.
- ignoreDirs is not null; an empty list means no ignored directories.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- The result is based on exact component matching rather than substring matching.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb
Summary:
Reads a file identified by a Path as UTF-8 text only if its size is within the
configured maximum size; otherwise it is skipped.
Algorithm:
1. Convert the Path to a readable filesystem target.
2. Check the file size against maxSizeMb.
3. If too large, skip the read.
4. Otherwise read UTF-8 content.
5. Return the content on success; otherwise return an empty string or a skip
   indication as implemented by the reader boundary.
Related Features:
- readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb(...)
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- filePath: A file path.
- maxSizeMb: Maximum allowed file size in megabytes.
Output:
- String: file content if successful; otherwise empty or skipped content.
Pre-condition:
- filePath is not null.
- maxSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- The method does not throw for normal unreadable/oversized-file cases; those
  cases are handled by skipping.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createOptions
Summary:
Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions instance from the supplied conversion
parameters.
Algorithm:
1. Initialize a new options object.
2. Assign directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, and
   maxFileSizeMb.
3. If ignoreDirs is null or empty, infer a default ignore list or leave it empty
   depending on calling policy; requirement coverage prefers default directory
   ignoring when conversion is used.
4. Return the populated options.
Related Features:
- createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input:
- directoryPath: Root directory to traverse.
- outputFile: Target Markdown file path.
- fileTypes: Optional list of lowercase file extensions including leading dot.
- ignoreDirs: Optional list of directory names to skip by component match.
- recursive: Whether nested directories are traversed.
- maxFileSizeMb: Maximum readable file size in megabytes.
Output:
- DirectoryToMarkdownOptions: a configured options object.
Pre-condition:
- directoryPath and outputFile are not null.
- maxFileSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- The returned object preserves the supplied configuration values.
- If defaults are applied, ignoreDirs contains the standard ignored directories.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb
Summary:
Reads a file identified by a string path as UTF-8 text only if its size is
within the configured maximum size; otherwise skips it with an appropriate
skip reason.
Algorithm:
1. Convert the string path to a file-system path.
2. Obtain the file size in bytes.
3. Compute the size limit in bytes from maxSizeMb.
4. If file size exceeds the limit, skip with TOO_LARGE.
5. Otherwise attempt to read the file as UTF-8 text.
6. If reading succeeds, return the content; if reading fails, skip with
   UNREADABLE.
Related Features:
- readFileSafelyFromString(...)
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- filePath: String representation of a file path.
- maxSizeMb: Maximum allowed file size in megabytes.
Output:
- String: UTF-8 file content if readable and not too large; otherwise an empty
  string or skipped result depending on implementation boundary.
Pre-condition:
- filePath is not null.
- maxSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- Large or unreadable files are not used as successful content.

DirectoryToMarkdown::convert
Summary:
Converts the directory tree described in the provided options into a single
Markdown document and returns the number of processed files.
Algorithm:
1. Validate options and resolve the root directory, output file, recursion flag,
   file type filters, ignored directories, and size limit.
2. Create or initialize a MarkdownConversionResult.
3. Open the output file for writing using a BufferedWriter.
4. Traverse the directory starting from options.directoryPath:
   - Use stable ordering for directory entries to guarantee deterministic output.
   - Respect recursion setting.
   - Skip ignored directories by path component.
   - For each eligible file, check file extension filter and file size limit.
   - Attempt UTF-8 reading; skip unreadable files.
   - For readable files, create a MarkdownFileEntry and format/write it.
   - Add each processed entry to the result.
5. Close the writer.
6. Return the processed file count from the result.
Related Features:
- directoryFilter
- fileContentReader
- markdownWriter
- createOptions(...)
- createDefaultOptions()
- walkDirectory(...)
- createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
- shouldIncludeExtension(...)
- getExtension(...)
- MarkdownConversionResult.processedFileCount
- MarkdownConversionResult.processedFiles
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, FileContentReader, MarkdownWriter, DirectoryFilter
Input:
- options: Conversion settings including root directory, output file, file type
  filter list, ignored directory names, recursion flag, and maximum file size in MB.
- fileTypes entries are lowercase extensions including the leading dot; null or
  empty list means no filtering.
- maxFileSizeMb is expected to be non-negative; inferred default behavior is to
  treat non-positive values as "no size limit" only if implemented consistently,
  otherwise as a zero-byte threshold.
Output:
- int: the number of files successfully processed and added to the Markdown result.
Pre-condition:
- options is not null.
- options.directoryPath and options.outputFile refer to valid filesystem paths.
- The root directory exists and is readable.
- The output location is writable.
- If file types are provided, they follow the lowercase-with-leading-dot format.
Post-condition:
- The output file contains a Markdown document representing all eligible,
  readable files encountered during traversal.
- Files are written in deterministic stable order.
- The returned count equals the number of entries stored in the conversion result.
- Empty or fully filtered directories produce an empty Markdown document.

DirectoryToMarkdown::shouldIncludeExtension
Summary:
Determines whether a file extension should be included based on an optional
file type filter list.
Algorithm:
1. If fileTypes is null or empty, return true.
2. Normalize extension to lowercase if needed.
3. Return true if extension exactly matches one of the fileTypes entries.
4. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- getExtension(path : Path) : String
- createOptions(...)
Related Classes: None
Input:
- extension: File extension including the leading dot, or empty string for files
  without extension.
- fileTypes: Optional list of lowercase extensions including the leading dot.
Output:
- boolean: true if the extension is eligible for processing; otherwise false.
Pre-condition:
- extension is not null.
- fileTypes, if present, contain extension strings in the expected format.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- When fileTypes is absent or empty, all regular files are considered eligible.

DirectoryToMarkdown::getExtension
Summary:
Extracts the file extension from a path as a lowercase string including the
leading dot, or returns an empty string when no extension exists.
Algorithm:
1. Obtain the last path segment from the input path.
2. Find the final '.' character in the file name.
3. If found and not the first character of the name, return the substring from
   that dot to the end, converted to lowercase.
4. Otherwise return an empty string.
Related Features:
- shouldIncludeExtension(extension : String, fileTypes : List<String>) : boolean
- createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
Related Classes: None
Input:
- path: A file path.
Output:
- String: The extension in lowercase including the leading dot, or an empty
  string if no extension is present.
Pre-condition:
- path is not null.
Post-condition:
- The returned extension can be directly compared with file type filters.

DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary:
Traverses a directory tree, filters eligible files, creates Markdown entries,
and writes them to the provided Markdown writer stream in stable order.
Algorithm:
1. List the entries in currentDir and sort them deterministically.
2. For each entry:
   - If it is a directory:
     a. If recursive is false, skip it.
     b. If the relative path component is ignored, skip it.
     c. Otherwise recurse into it.
   - If it is a file:
     a. Compute its relative path against baseDir.
     b. Skip if any relative-path component is ignored.
     c. Derive its extension using getExtension(...).
     d. Skip if shouldIncludeExtension(...) returns false.
     e. Read file content through fileContentReader with max size limit.
     f. If unreadable or too large, skip.
     g. Create a MarkdownFileEntry and format it with markdownWriter.
     h. Write the formatted section to md.
     i. Add the entry to result using addProcessedFile(...).
3. Return.
Related Features:
- getExtension(...)
- isIgnored(...)
- shouldIncludeExtension(...)
- createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
- fileContentReader
- markdownWriter
- MarkdownConversionResult.addProcessedFile(...)
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, FileContentReader, MarkdownWriter, FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- currentDir: Directory currently being traversed.
- md: BufferedWriter for the Markdown output.
- optons: Conversion options.
- baseDir: Root directory used to compute relative paths.
- result: Accumulator for processed Markdown entries.
Output:
- void
Pre-condition:
- currentDir exists and is readable.
- md is open for writing.
- optons, baseDir, and result are not null.
Post-condition:
- All eligible files reachable from currentDir under the traversal rules are
  written exactly once.
- result is updated consistently with every written entry.
- Traversal respects recursion, ignore rules, type filters, and size limits.

DirectoryToMarkdown::directoryToMarkdown
Summary:
Convenience wrapper that converts a directory to Markdown using the raw
parameter list and returns the count of processed files.
Algorithm:
1. Call createOptions(...) with the supplied parameters.
2. Pass the resulting options to convert(...).
3. Return the integer result from convert(...).
Related Features:
- createOptions(...)
- convert(...)
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input:
- directoryPath: Root directory to convert.
- outputFile: Markdown output file.
- fileTypes: Optional extension filter list.
- ignoreDirs: Optional ignored directory names.
- recursive: True to scan nested directories; false for only direct children.
- maxFileSizeMb: Maximum readable file size in MB.
Output:
- int: number of processed files.
Pre-condition:
- Same preconditions as createOptions(...) and convert(...).
Post-condition:
- A Markdown output file is produced if at least one eligible file exists.
- The return value matches the processed file count of the conversion.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createDefaultOptions
Summary:
Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions instance populated with the system's default
settings for ignored directories and other conversion parameters.
Algorithm:
1. Initialize an options object.
2. Set default ignored directory names using createDefaultIgnoreDirs().
3. Set other fields to inferred safe defaults:
   - empty directory path/output file placeholders,
   - no file type restriction,
   - recursive enabled,
   - a reasonable default maximum file size in MB.
4. Return the populated options object.
Related Features:
- createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input:
- None.
Output:
- DirectoryToMarkdownOptions: an options object configured with defaults.
Pre-condition:
- None.
Post-condition:
- The returned options contains a non-null ignoreDirs list with the default
  ignored directory names.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createMarkdownFileEntry
Summary:
Creates a MarkdownFileEntry from a file's relative path, extension, and textual
content, including the derived Markdown language label.
Algorithm:
1. Normalize relativePath to a string representation suitable for Markdown.
2. Set extension as provided.
3. Derive language from extension by removing the leading dot if present.
4. Store content unchanged.
5. Return the new MarkdownFileEntry.
Related Features:
- getExtension(path : Path) : String
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input:
- relativePath: Relative path of the file from the root directory.
- extension: File extension including the leading dot, or empty string if none.
- content: UTF-8 textual content of the file.
Output:
- MarkdownFileEntry: an entry containing relativePath, extension, language, and
  content.
Pre-condition:
- relativePath is not null.
- content is not null.
Post-condition:
- The returned entry is ready to be formatted as a Markdown section.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

```plantuml
    NONE
    TOO_LARGE
    UNREADABLE
}
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::isIgnored
Summary:
Checks whether a relative path should be ignored because any of its components
matches one of the configured ignored directory names.
Algorithm:
1. Split the path into components.
2. Compare each component for exact equality with each entry in ignoreDirs.
3. Return true on the first match.
4. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- directoryFilter
- createOptions(...)
Related Classes: None
Input:
- path: Relative or absolute path string.
- ignoreDirs: List of directory names to ignore.
Output:
- boolean: true if the path contains an ignored component; otherwise false.
Pre-condition:
- path is not null.
- ignoreDirs is not null; an empty list means no ignored directories.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- The result is based on exact component matching rather than substring matching.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb
Summary:
Reads a file identified by a Path as UTF-8 text only if its size is within the
configured maximum size; otherwise it is skipped.
Algorithm:
1. Convert the Path to a readable filesystem target.
2. Check the file size against maxSizeMb.
3. If too large, skip the read.
4. Otherwise read UTF-8 content.
5. Return the content on success; otherwise return an empty string or a skip
   indication as implemented by the reader boundary.
Related Features:
- readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb(...)
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- filePath: A file path.
- maxSizeMb: Maximum allowed file size in megabytes.
Output:
- String: file content if successful; otherwise empty or skipped content.
Pre-condition:
- filePath is not null.
- maxSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- The method does not throw for normal unreadable/oversized-file cases; those
  cases are handled by skipping.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createOptions
Summary:
Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions instance from the supplied conversion
parameters.
Algorithm:
1. Initialize a new options object.
2. Assign directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, and
   maxFileSizeMb.
3. If ignoreDirs is null or empty, infer a default ignore list or leave it empty
   depending on calling policy; requirement coverage prefers default directory
   ignoring when conversion is used.
4. Return the populated options.
Related Features:
- createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input:
- directoryPath: Root directory to traverse.
- outputFile: Target Markdown file path.
- fileTypes: Optional list of lowercase file extensions including leading dot.
- ignoreDirs: Optional list of directory names to skip by component match.
- recursive: Whether nested directories are traversed.
- maxFileSizeMb: Maximum readable file size in megabytes.
Output:
- DirectoryToMarkdownOptions: a configured options object.
Pre-condition:
- directoryPath and outputFile are not null.
- maxFileSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- The returned object preserves the supplied configuration values.
- If defaults are applied, ignoreDirs contains the standard ignored directories.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb
Summary:
Reads a file identified by a string path as UTF-8 text only if its size is
within the configured maximum size; otherwise skips it with an appropriate
skip reason.
Algorithm:
1. Convert the string path to a file-system path.
2. Obtain the file size in bytes.
3. Compute the size limit in bytes from maxSizeMb.
4. If file size exceeds the limit, skip with TOO_LARGE.
5. Otherwise attempt to read the file as UTF-8 text.
6. If reading succeeds, return the content; if reading fails, skip with
   UNREADABLE.
Related Features:
- readFileSafelyFromString(...)
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- filePath: String representation of a file path.
- maxSizeMb: Maximum allowed file size in megabytes.
Output:
- String: UTF-8 file content if readable and not too large; otherwise an empty
  string or skipped result depending on implementation boundary.
Pre-condition:
- filePath is not null.
- maxSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- Large or unreadable files are not used as successful content.

DirectoryToMarkdown::convert
Summary:
Converts the directory tree described in the provided options into a single
Markdown document and returns the number of processed files.
Algorithm:
1. Validate options and resolve the root directory, output file, recursion flag,
   file type filters, ignored directories, and size limit.
2. Create or initialize a MarkdownConversionResult.
3. Open the output file for writing using a BufferedWriter.
4. Traverse the directory starting from options.directoryPath:
   - Use stable ordering for directory entries to guarantee deterministic output.
   - Respect recursion setting.
   - Skip ignored directories by path component.
   - For each eligible file, check file extension filter and file size limit.
   - Attempt UTF-8 reading; skip unreadable files.
   - For readable files, create a MarkdownFileEntry and format/write it.
   - Add each processed entry to the result.
5. Close the writer.
6. Return the processed file count from the result.
Related Features:
- directoryFilter
- fileContentReader
- markdownWriter
- createOptions(...)
- createDefaultOptions()
- walkDirectory(...)
- createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
- shouldIncludeExtension(...)
- getExtension(...)
- MarkdownConversionResult.processedFileCount
- MarkdownConversionResult.processedFiles
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, FileContentReader, MarkdownWriter, DirectoryFilter
Input:
- options: Conversion settings including root directory, output file, file type
  filter list, ignored directory names, recursion flag, and maximum file size in MB.
- fileTypes entries are lowercase extensions including the leading dot; null or
  empty list means no filtering.
- maxFileSizeMb is expected to be non-negative; inferred default behavior is to
  treat non-positive values as "no size limit" only if implemented consistently,
  otherwise as a zero-byte threshold.
Output:
- int: the number of files successfully processed and added to the Markdown result.
Pre-condition:
- options is not null.
- options.directoryPath and options.outputFile refer to valid filesystem paths.
- The root directory exists and is readable.
- The output location is writable.
- If file types are provided, they follow the lowercase-with-leading-dot format.
Post-condition:
- The output file contains a Markdown document representing all eligible,
  readable files encountered during traversal.
- Files are written in deterministic stable order.
- The returned count equals the number of entries stored in the conversion result.
- Empty or fully filtered directories produce an empty Markdown document.

DirectoryToMarkdown::shouldIncludeExtension
Summary:
Determines whether a file extension should be included based on an optional
file type filter list.
Algorithm:
1. If fileTypes is null or empty, return true.
2. Normalize extension to lowercase if needed.
3. Return true if extension exactly matches one of the fileTypes entries.
4. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- getExtension(path : Path) : String
- createOptions(...)
Related Classes: None
Input:
- extension: File extension including the leading dot, or empty string for files
  without extension.
- fileTypes: Optional list of lowercase extensions including the leading dot.
Output:
- boolean: true if the extension is eligible for processing; otherwise false.
Pre-condition:
- extension is not null.
- fileTypes, if present, contain extension strings in the expected format.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- When fileTypes is absent or empty, all regular files are considered eligible.

DirectoryToMarkdown::getExtension
Summary:
Extracts the file extension from a path as a lowercase string including the
leading dot, or returns an empty string when no extension exists.
Algorithm:
1. Obtain the last path segment from the input path.
2. Find the final '.' character in the file name.
3. If found and not the first character of the name, return the substring from
   that dot to the end, converted to lowercase.
4. Otherwise return an empty string.
Related Features:
- shouldIncludeExtension(extension : String, fileTypes : List<String>) : boolean
- createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
Related Classes: None
Input:
- path: A file path.
Output:
- String: The extension in lowercase including the leading dot, or an empty
  string if no extension is present.
Pre-condition:
- path is not null.
Post-condition:
- The returned extension can be directly compared with file type filters.

DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary:
Traverses a directory tree, filters eligible files, creates Markdown entries,
and writes them to the provided Markdown writer stream in stable order.
Algorithm:
1. List the entries in currentDir and sort them deterministically.
2. For each entry:
   - If it is a directory:
     a. If recursive is false, skip it.
     b. If the relative path component is ignored, skip it.
     c. Otherwise recurse into it.
   - If it is a file:
     a. Compute its relative path against baseDir.
     b. Skip if any relative-path component is ignored.
     c. Derive its extension using getExtension(...).
     d. Skip if shouldIncludeExtension(...) returns false.
     e. Read file content through fileContentReader with max size limit.
     f. If unreadable or too large, skip.
     g. Create a MarkdownFileEntry and format it with markdownWriter.
     h. Write the formatted section to md.
     i. Add the entry to result using addProcessedFile(...).
3. Return.
Related Features:
- getExtension(...)
- isIgnored(...)
- shouldIncludeExtension(...)
- createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
- fileContentReader
- markdownWriter
- MarkdownConversionResult.addProcessedFile(...)
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, FileContentReader, MarkdownWriter, FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- currentDir: Directory currently being traversed.
- md: BufferedWriter for the Markdown output.
- optons: Conversion options.
- baseDir: Root directory used to compute relative paths.
- result: Accumulator for processed Markdown entries.
Output:
- void
Pre-condition:
- currentDir exists and is readable.
- md is open for writing.
- optons, baseDir, and result are not null.
Post-condition:
- All eligible files reachable from currentDir under the traversal rules are
  written exactly once.
- result is updated consistently with every written entry.
- Traversal respects recursion, ignore rules, type filters, and size limits.

DirectoryToMarkdown::directoryToMarkdown
Summary:
Convenience wrapper that converts a directory to Markdown using the raw
parameter list and returns the count of processed files.
Algorithm:
1. Call createOptions(...) with the supplied parameters.
2. Pass the resulting options to convert(...).
3. Return the integer result from convert(...).
Related Features:
- createOptions(...)
- convert(...)
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input:
- directoryPath: Root directory to convert.
- outputFile: Markdown output file.
- fileTypes: Optional extension filter list.
- ignoreDirs: Optional ignored directory names.
- recursive: True to scan nested directories; false for only direct children.
- maxFileSizeMb: Maximum readable file size in MB.
Output:
- int: number of processed files.
Pre-condition:
- Same preconditions as createOptions(...) and convert(...).
Post-condition:
- A Markdown output file is produced if at least one eligible file exists.
- The return value matches the processed file count of the conversion.

```plantuml
    ignoreDirs : List<String>
    maxFileSizeMb : int
    outputFile : String
    recursive : boolean
}
class FileContentReader {
    readFileSafely(filePath : Path, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
    readFileSafelyFromString(filePath : String, maxSizeMb : int) : FileReadResult
}
class FileReadResult {
    content : String
    readable : boolean
    skippedReason : SkipReason
}
class MarkdownConversionResult {
    processedFileCount : int
    processedFiles : List<MarkdownFileEntry>
    addProcessedFile(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : void
}
class MarkdownFileEntry {
    content : String
    extension : String
    language : String
    relativePath : String
}
class MarkdownWriter {
    formatEntry(entry : MarkdownFileEntry) : String
}
enum SkipReason {
    NONE
    TOO_LARGE
    UNREADABLE
}

DirectoryToMarkdown --> DirectoryFilter : directoryFilter
DirectoryToMarkdown --> FileContentReader : fileContentReader
DirectoryToMarkdown --> MarkdownWriter : markdownWriter
MarkdownConversionResult *-- "*" MarkdownFileEntry : processedFiles
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
DirectoryToMarkdown::isIgnored
Summary:
Checks whether a relative path should be ignored because any of its components
matches one of the configured ignored directory names.
Algorithm:
1. Split the path into components.
2. Compare each component for exact equality with each entry in ignoreDirs.
3. Return true on the first match.
4. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- directoryFilter
- createOptions(...)
Related Classes: None
Input:
- path: Relative or absolute path string.
- ignoreDirs: List of directory names to ignore.
Output:
- boolean: true if the path contains an ignored component; otherwise false.
Pre-condition:
- path is not null.
- ignoreDirs is not null; an empty list means no ignored directories.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- The result is based on exact component matching rather than substring matching.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyfromPathSizeMb
Summary:
Reads a file identified by a Path as UTF-8 text only if its size is within the
configured maximum size; otherwise it is skipped.
Algorithm:
1. Convert the Path to a readable filesystem target.
2. Check the file size against maxSizeMb.
3. If too large, skip the read.
4. Otherwise read UTF-8 content.
5. Return the content on success; otherwise return an empty string or a skip
   indication as implemented by the reader boundary.
Related Features:
- readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb(...)
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- filePath: A file path.
- maxSizeMb: Maximum allowed file size in megabytes.
Output:
- String: file content if successful; otherwise empty or skipped content.
Pre-condition:
- filePath is not null.
- maxSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- The method does not throw for normal unreadable/oversized-file cases; those
  cases are handled by skipping.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createOptions
Summary:
Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions instance from the supplied conversion
parameters.
Algorithm:
1. Initialize a new options object.
2. Assign directoryPath, outputFile, fileTypes, ignoreDirs, recursive, and
   maxFileSizeMb.
3. If ignoreDirs is null or empty, infer a default ignore list or leave it empty
   depending on calling policy; requirement coverage prefers default directory
   ignoring when conversion is used.
4. Return the populated options.
Related Features:
- createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input:
- directoryPath: Root directory to traverse.
- outputFile: Target Markdown file path.
- fileTypes: Optional list of lowercase file extensions including leading dot.
- ignoreDirs: Optional list of directory names to skip by component match.
- recursive: Whether nested directories are traversed.
- maxFileSizeMb: Maximum readable file size in megabytes.
Output:
- DirectoryToMarkdownOptions: a configured options object.
Pre-condition:
- directoryPath and outputFile are not null.
- maxFileSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- The returned object preserves the supplied configuration values.
- If defaults are applied, ignoreDirs contains the standard ignored directories.

DirectoryToMarkdown::readFileSafelyFromStringSizeMb
Summary:
Reads a file identified by a string path as UTF-8 text only if its size is
within the configured maximum size; otherwise skips it with an appropriate
skip reason.
Algorithm:
1. Convert the string path to a file-system path.
2. Obtain the file size in bytes.
3. Compute the size limit in bytes from maxSizeMb.
4. If file size exceeds the limit, skip with TOO_LARGE.
5. Otherwise attempt to read the file as UTF-8 text.
6. If reading succeeds, return the content; if reading fails, skip with
   UNREADABLE.
Related Features:
- readFileSafelyFromString(...)
Related Classes: FileContentReader, FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- filePath: String representation of a file path.
- maxSizeMb: Maximum allowed file size in megabytes.
Output:
- String: UTF-8 file content if readable and not too large; otherwise an empty
  string or skipped result depending on implementation boundary.
Pre-condition:
- filePath is not null.
- maxSizeMb is non-negative.
Post-condition:
- Large or unreadable files are not used as successful content.

DirectoryToMarkdown::convert
Summary:
Converts the directory tree described in the provided options into a single
Markdown document and returns the number of processed files.
Algorithm:
1. Validate options and resolve the root directory, output file, recursion flag,
   file type filters, ignored directories, and size limit.
2. Create or initialize a MarkdownConversionResult.
3. Open the output file for writing using a BufferedWriter.
4. Traverse the directory starting from options.directoryPath:
   - Use stable ordering for directory entries to guarantee deterministic output.
   - Respect recursion setting.
   - Skip ignored directories by path component.
   - For each eligible file, check file extension filter and file size limit.
   - Attempt UTF-8 reading; skip unreadable files.
   - For readable files, create a MarkdownFileEntry and format/write it.
   - Add each processed entry to the result.
5. Close the writer.
6. Return the processed file count from the result.
Related Features:
- directoryFilter
- fileContentReader
- markdownWriter
- createOptions(...)
- createDefaultOptions()
- walkDirectory(...)
- createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
- shouldIncludeExtension(...)
- getExtension(...)
- MarkdownConversionResult.processedFileCount
- MarkdownConversionResult.processedFiles
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, FileContentReader, MarkdownWriter, DirectoryFilter
Input:
- options: Conversion settings including root directory, output file, file type
  filter list, ignored directory names, recursion flag, and maximum file size in MB.
- fileTypes entries are lowercase extensions including the leading dot; null or
  empty list means no filtering.
- maxFileSizeMb is expected to be non-negative; inferred default behavior is to
  treat non-positive values as "no size limit" only if implemented consistently,
  otherwise as a zero-byte threshold.
Output:
- int: the number of files successfully processed and added to the Markdown result.
Pre-condition:
- options is not null.
- options.directoryPath and options.outputFile refer to valid filesystem paths.
- The root directory exists and is readable.
- The output location is writable.
- If file types are provided, they follow the lowercase-with-leading-dot format.
Post-condition:
- The output file contains a Markdown document representing all eligible,
  readable files encountered during traversal.
- Files are written in deterministic stable order.
- The returned count equals the number of entries stored in the conversion result.
- Empty or fully filtered directories produce an empty Markdown document.

DirectoryToMarkdown::shouldIncludeExtension
Summary:
Determines whether a file extension should be included based on an optional
file type filter list.
Algorithm:
1. If fileTypes is null or empty, return true.
2. Normalize extension to lowercase if needed.
3. Return true if extension exactly matches one of the fileTypes entries.
4. Otherwise return false.
Related Features:
- getExtension(path : Path) : String
- createOptions(...)
Related Classes: None
Input:
- extension: File extension including the leading dot, or empty string for files
  without extension.
- fileTypes: Optional list of lowercase extensions including the leading dot.
Output:
- boolean: true if the extension is eligible for processing; otherwise false.
Pre-condition:
- extension is not null.
- fileTypes, if present, contain extension strings in the expected format.
Post-condition:
- No state is modified.
- When fileTypes is absent or empty, all regular files are considered eligible.

DirectoryToMarkdown::getExtension
Summary:
Extracts the file extension from a path as a lowercase string including the
leading dot, or returns an empty string when no extension exists.
Algorithm:
1. Obtain the last path segment from the input path.
2. Find the final '.' character in the file name.
3. If found and not the first character of the name, return the substring from
   that dot to the end, converted to lowercase.
4. Otherwise return an empty string.
Related Features:
- shouldIncludeExtension(extension : String, fileTypes : List<String>) : boolean
- createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
Related Classes: None
Input:
- path: A file path.
Output:
- String: The extension in lowercase including the leading dot, or an empty
  string if no extension is present.
Pre-condition:
- path is not null.
Post-condition:
- The returned extension can be directly compared with file type filters.

DirectoryToMarkdown::walkDirectory
Summary:
Traverses a directory tree, filters eligible files, creates Markdown entries,
and writes them to the provided Markdown writer stream in stable order.
Algorithm:
1. List the entries in currentDir and sort them deterministically.
2. For each entry:
   - If it is a directory:
     a. If recursive is false, skip it.
     b. If the relative path component is ignored, skip it.
     c. Otherwise recurse into it.
   - If it is a file:
     a. Compute its relative path against baseDir.
     b. Skip if any relative-path component is ignored.
     c. Derive its extension using getExtension(...).
     d. Skip if shouldIncludeExtension(...) returns false.
     e. Read file content through fileContentReader with max size limit.
     f. If unreadable or too large, skip.
     g. Create a MarkdownFileEntry and format it with markdownWriter.
     h. Write the formatted section to md.
     i. Add the entry to result using addProcessedFile(...).
3. Return.
Related Features:
- getExtension(...)
- isIgnored(...)
- shouldIncludeExtension(...)
- createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
- fileContentReader
- markdownWriter
- MarkdownConversionResult.addProcessedFile(...)
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, FileContentReader, MarkdownWriter, FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- currentDir: Directory currently being traversed.
- md: BufferedWriter for the Markdown output.
- optons: Conversion options.
- baseDir: Root directory used to compute relative paths.
- result: Accumulator for processed Markdown entries.
Output:
- void
Pre-condition:
- currentDir exists and is readable.
- md is open for writing.
- optons, baseDir, and result are not null.
Post-condition:
- All eligible files reachable from currentDir under the traversal rules are
  written exactly once.
- result is updated consistently with every written entry.
- Traversal respects recursion, ignore rules, type filters, and size limits.

DirectoryToMarkdown::directoryToMarkdown
Summary:
Convenience wrapper that converts a directory to Markdown using the raw
parameter list and returns the count of processed files.
Algorithm:
1. Call createOptions(...) with the supplied parameters.
2. Pass the resulting options to convert(...).
3. Return the integer result from convert(...).
Related Features:
- createOptions(...)
- convert(...)
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input:
- directoryPath: Root directory to convert.
- outputFile: Markdown output file.
- fileTypes: Optional extension filter list.
- ignoreDirs: Optional ignored directory names.
- recursive: True to scan nested directories; false for only direct children.
- maxFileSizeMb: Maximum readable file size in MB.
Output:
- int: number of processed files.
Pre-condition:
- Same preconditions as createOptions(...) and convert(...).
Post-condition:
- A Markdown output file is produced if at least one eligible file exists.
- The return value matches the processed file count of the conversion.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createDefaultOptions
Summary:
Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions instance populated with the system's default
settings for ignored directories and other conversion parameters.
Algorithm:
1. Initialize an options object.
2. Set default ignored directory names using createDefaultIgnoreDirs().
3. Set other fields to inferred safe defaults:
   - empty directory path/output file placeholders,
   - no file type restriction,
   - recursive enabled,
   - a reasonable default maximum file size in MB.
4. Return the populated options object.
Related Features:
- createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input:
- None.
Output:
- DirectoryToMarkdownOptions: an options object configured with defaults.
Pre-condition:
- None.
Post-condition:
- The returned options contains a non-null ignoreDirs list with the default
  ignored directory names.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createMarkdownFileEntry
Summary:
Creates a MarkdownFileEntry from a file's relative path, extension, and textual
content, including the derived Markdown language label.
Algorithm:
1. Normalize relativePath to a string representation suitable for Markdown.
2. Set extension as provided.
3. Derive language from extension by removing the leading dot if present.
4. Store content unchanged.
5. Return the new MarkdownFileEntry.
Related Features:
- getExtension(path : Path) : String
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input:
- relativePath: Relative path of the file from the root directory.
- extension: File extension including the leading dot, or empty string if none.
- content: UTF-8 textual content of the file.
Output:
- MarkdownFileEntry: an entry containing relativePath, extension, language, and
  content.
Pre-condition:
- relativePath is not null.
- content is not null.
Post-condition:
- The returned entry is ready to be formatted as a Markdown section.

# Requirement
```text
# Requirement: joseph-crowley_directory-to-markdown

## 1. Overall / Domain Description

This system converts the contents of a directory into a single Markdown document. It is intended for collecting source files or text files into a readable Markdown bundle while respecting file type filters, ignored directories, recursion settings, and file size limits. The conversion should preserve each processed file's relative path and textual content.

## 2. Functional Requirement

Directory traversal starts from a configured root directory and writes to a configured output file. When recursive mode is enabled, nested directories should be scanned; otherwise, only files directly under the root are considered. Directory entries should be handled in a stable order so repeated conversions produce deterministic Markdown output.

Ignored directories are matched by path component rather than by substring. A file is skipped when any component of its relative path is one of the ignored directory names. The default ignored directory names are `node_modules`, `__pycache__`, `.git`, `.vscode`, and `.idea`.

File type filtering is based on lowercase file extensions including the leading dot. If no file type filter is provided, all regular files are eligible. Files larger than the configured maximum size in megabytes should not be read. Unreadable files should also be skipped. Skip reasons distinguish successful reads, files that are too large, and files that cannot be read.

Readable files are interpreted as UTF-8 text. Each processed file becomes one Markdown section. The section heading should be `## <relativePath>`, followed by a fenced code block. The code block language is derived from the file extension without the leading dot. Empty directories or directories with no eligible files should produce an empty Markdown document.

The conversion result should track both the number of processed files and the corresponding file entries. Adding an entry should keep this count consistent with the stored entries.

## 3. External / Tool Classes

There is no helper class required outside the UML model.
```

   - If it is a file:
     a. Compute its relative path against baseDir.
     b. Skip if any relative-path component is ignored.
     c. Derive its extension using getExtension(...).
     d. Skip if shouldIncludeExtension(...) returns false.
     e. Read file content through fileContentReader with max size limit.
     f. If unreadable or too large, skip.
     g. Create a MarkdownFileEntry and format it with markdownWriter.
     h. Write the formatted section to md.
     i. Add the entry to result using addProcessedFile(...).
3. Return.
Related Features:
- getExtension(...)
- isIgnored(...)
- shouldIncludeExtension(...)
- createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
- fileContentReader
- markdownWriter
- MarkdownConversionResult.addProcessedFile(...)
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, FileContentReader, MarkdownWriter, FileReadResult, SkipReason
Input:
- currentDir: Directory currently being traversed.
- md: BufferedWriter for the Markdown output.
- optons: Conversion options.
- baseDir: Root directory used to compute relative paths.
- result: Accumulator for processed Markdown entries.
Output:
- void
Pre-condition:
- currentDir exists and is readable.
- md is open for writing.
- optons, baseDir, and result are not null.
Post-condition:
- All eligible files reachable from currentDir under the traversal rules are
  written exactly once.
- result is updated consistently with every written entry.
- Traversal respects recursion, ignore rules, type filters, and size limits.

DirectoryToMarkdown::directoryToMarkdown
Summary:
Convenience wrapper that converts a directory to Markdown using the raw
parameter list and returns the count of processed files.
Algorithm:
1. Call createOptions(...) with the supplied parameters.
2. Pass the resulting options to convert(...).
3. Return the integer result from convert(...).
Related Features:
- createOptions(...)
- convert(...)
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input:
- directoryPath: Root directory to convert.
- outputFile: Markdown output file.
- fileTypes: Optional extension filter list.
- ignoreDirs: Optional ignored directory names.
- recursive: True to scan nested directories; false for only direct children.
- maxFileSizeMb: Maximum readable file size in MB.
Output:
- int: number of processed files.
Pre-condition:
- Same preconditions as createOptions(...) and convert(...).
Post-condition:
- A Markdown output file is produced if at least one eligible file exists.
- The return value matches the processed file count of the conversion.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createDefaultOptions
Summary:
Creates a DirectoryToMarkdownOptions instance populated with the system's default
settings for ignored directories and other conversion parameters.
Algorithm:
1. Initialize an options object.
2. Set default ignored directory names using createDefaultIgnoreDirs().
3. Set other fields to inferred safe defaults:
   - empty directory path/output file placeholders,
   - no file type restriction,
   - recursive enabled,
   - a reasonable default maximum file size in MB.
4. Return the populated options object.
Related Features:
- createDefaultIgnoreDirs()
Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions
Input:
- None.
Output:
- DirectoryToMarkdownOptions: an options object configured with defaults.
Pre-condition:
- None.
Post-condition:
- The returned options contains a non-null ignoreDirs list with the default
  ignored directory names.

DirectoryToMarkdown::createMarkdownFileEntry
Summary:
Creates a MarkdownFileEntry from a file's relative path, extension, and textual
content, including the derived Markdown language label.
Algorithm:
1. Normalize relativePath to a string representation suitable for Markdown.
2. Set extension as provided.
3. Derive language from extension by removing the leading dot if present.
4. Store content unchanged.
5. Return the new MarkdownFileEntry.
Related Features:
- getExtension(path : Path) : String
Related Classes: MarkdownFileEntry
Input:
- relativePath: Relative path of the file from the root directory.
- extension: File extension including the leading dot, or empty string if none.
- content: UTF-8 textual content of the file.
Output:
- MarkdownFileEntry: an entry containing relativePath, extension, language, and
  content.
Pre-condition:
- relativePath is not null.
- content is not null.
Post-condition:
- The returned entry is ready to be formatted as a Markdown section.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information

  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.

  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.

  3.3 You should not change the method signature;

4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

Complete this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

    final long maxBytes = (long) maxSizeMb * 1024L * 1024L;

    try
    {
      if (!java.nio.file.Files.exists(filePath) || !java.nio.file.Files.isReadable(filePath))
      {
        result.setReadable(false);
        result.setSkippedReason(SkipReason.UNREADABLE);
        result.setContent(null);
        return result;
      }

      final long size = java.nio.file.Files.size(filePath);
      if (size > maxBytes)
      {
        result.setReadable(false);
        result.setSkippedReason(SkipReason.TOO_LARGE);
        result.setContent(null);
        return result;
      }

      result.setContent(java.nio.file.Files.readString(filePath, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

      result.setSkippedReason(SkipReason.NONE);

1. Compute context information
  1.1 Consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.2 Locate these files in your project.
  1.3 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the context.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

```java
   * Summary:
```

```java
  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
```
```java
   * Traverses a directory tree, filters eligible files, creates Markdown entries,
   * and writes them to the provided Markdown writer stream in stable order.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. List the entries in currentDir and sort them deterministically.
   * 2. For each entry:
   *    - If it is a directory:
   *      a. If recursive is false, skip it.
   *      b. If the relative path component is ignored, skip it.
   *      c. Otherwise recurse into it.
   *    - If it is a file:
   *      a. Compute its relative path against baseDir.
   *      b. Skip if any relative-path component is ignored.
   *      c. Derive its extension using getExtension(...).
   *      d. Skip if shouldIncludeExtension(...) returns false.
   *      e. Read file content through fileContentReader with max size limit.
   *      f. If unreadable or too large, skip.
   *      g. Create a MarkdownFileEntry and format it with markdownWriter.
   *      h. Write the formatted section to md.
   *      i. Add the entry to result using addProcessedFile(...).
   * 3. Return.
   * Related Features:
   * - getExtension(...)
   * - isIgnored(...)
   * - shouldIncludeExtension(...)
   * - createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
   * - fileContentReader
   * - markdownWriter
   * - MarkdownConversionResult.addProcessedFile(...)
   * Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, FileContentReader, MarkdownWriter, FileReadResult, SkipReason
   * Input:
   * - currentDir: Directory currently being traversed.
   * - md: BufferedWriter for the Markdown output.
   * - optons: Conversion options.
   * - baseDir: Root directory used to compute relative paths.
   * - result: Accumulator for processed Markdown entries.
   * Output:
   * - void
   * Pre-condition:
   * - currentDir exists and is readable.
   * - md is open for writing.
   * - optons, baseDir, and result are not null.
   * Post-condition:
   * - All eligible files reachable from currentDir under the traversal rules are
   *   written exactly once.
   * - result is updated consistently with every written entry.
   * - Traversal respects recursion, ignore rules, type filters, and size limits.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated
```

```java
   *      f. If unreadable or too large, skip.
   *      g. Create a MarkdownFileEntry and format it with markdownWriter.
   *      h. Write the formatted section to md.

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
```
```java
   * Traverses a directory tree, filters eligible files, creates Markdown entries,
   * and writes them to the provided Markdown writer stream in stable order.
   * Algorithm:
   * 1. List the entries in currentDir and sort them deterministically.
   * 2. For each entry:
   *    - If it is a directory:
   *      a. If recursive is false, skip it.
   *      b. If the relative path component is ignored, skip it.
   *      c. Otherwise recurse into it.
   *    - If it is a file:
   *      a. Compute its relative path against baseDir.
   *      b. Skip if any relative-path component is ignored.
   *      c. Derive its extension using getExtension(...).
   *      d. Skip if shouldIncludeExtension(...) returns false.
   *      e. Read file content through fileContentReader with max size limit.
   *      f. If unreadable or too large, skip.
   *      g. Create a MarkdownFileEntry and format it with markdownWriter.
   *      h. Write the formatted section to md.
   *      i. Add the entry to result using addProcessedFile(...).
   * 3. Return.
   * Related Features:
   * - getExtension(...)
   * - isIgnored(...)
   * - shouldIncludeExtension(...)
   * - createMarkdownFileEntry(...)
   * - fileContentReader
   * - markdownWriter
   * - MarkdownConversionResult.addProcessedFile(...)
   * Related Classes: DirectoryToMarkdownOptions, MarkdownConversionResult, MarkdownFileEntry, FileContentReader, MarkdownWriter, FileReadResult, SkipReason
   * Input:
   * - currentDir: Directory currently being traversed.
   * - md: BufferedWriter for the Markdown output.
   * - optons: Conversion options.
   * - baseDir: Root directory used to compute relative paths.
   * - result: Accumulator for processed Markdown entries.
   * Output:
   * - void
   * Pre-condition:
   * - currentDir exists and is readable.
   * - md is open for writing.
   * - optons, baseDir, and result are not null.
   * Post-condition:
   * - All eligible files reachable from currentDir under the traversal rules are
   *   written exactly once.
   * - result is updated consistently with every written entry.
   * - Traversal respects recursion, ignore rules, type filters, and size limits.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model currentDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path" mdDataType="edu.joseph.BufferedWriter" baseDirDataType="edu.joseph.Path"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
```
